## Purpose

Record one terminal static semantic disposition for every accepted T0A source identity and one terminal provenance disposition for every hidden-clone declaration occurrence. This is worker evidence for independent review, not closure authority.

## Scope / Methodology

The read-only source root was `D:\UNG DUNG AI\TOOL AI 2026\CVF-Workspace\SOT-Application`. I enumerated all physical files including hidden paths, sorted normalized relative paths by ordinal code point, recomputed byte length and SHA-256, decoded every full body as UTF-8, and reconciled every result to the accepted T0A metadata row. Each semantic decision below is grounded in a body-derived observation and retains a file-specific adversarial challenge. No source-root file was changed and no runtime, build, test, browser, provider, server, or live-proof command was run.

## Findings / Position

- Snapshot verdict: `MATCH` at 336 files, 238522 bytes, aggregate SHA-256 `bbf4a91d7fb50134c711ffef8af2a6107105fc0aae9b341a0ca3896ce58534ee`.
- Full-body read verdict: 336 successful UTF-8 reads; zero unreadable, missing, duplicate, excluded, or unresolved identities.
- Calibration verdict: all 20 accepted T0A sample meanings are preserved within the 336-row ledger.
- Declaration verdict: DEC-01 through DEC-13 are terminally classified; DEC-05, DEC-06, and DEC-08 remain literal missing-target evidence.
- Final reviewer position: `ACCEPTED_BY_REVIEWER_WITH_REPAIRS` after one consolidated repair and independent re-audit.

The recovered provider-local execution log was used only as a continuity aid and is `NOT_CVF_SOURCE`. Its prior reads were independently superseded by fresh direct full-body reads of all 336 files in this invocation.

## Reviewer Repair Round 1

The independent reviewer returned `REPAIR_REQUIRED` after objective evidence and all structural gates passed. Reviewer-owned repairs were consolidated before any second review:

- all 316 non-sample `contentObservation`, `reason`, and `adversarialChallenge` cells are now individually body-grounded and unique; 302 challenges include the exact path and the remaining 14 are bespoke binding/script/CSS/ignore challenges with concrete symbols, literals, targets, or mutations;
- SRC-040/SRC-044 now distinguish `/audit` request/event/claim-boundary output from `/health` status and `governed=false` output;
- SRC-053/SRC-112 now distinguish API `outDir`/`rootDir` from Web JSX/DOM/`noEmit`/Vite configuration;
- SRC-300 through SRC-308 were individually reviewed: mutating bootstrap/export/migration/seed scripts are runtime candidates rejected for direct import, while structural validators and bounded proof harnesses carry checker-candidate value with explicit proof ceilings;
- the 23 non-sample tests were individually reviewed: 14 importing local production modules carry `CHECKER_CANDIDATE` value while remaining rejected as current CVF proof; nine literal-only failure tests retain `REJECT_DIRECT_IMPORT` with exact tautology/proof-strength challenges;
- SRC-019 `.gitignore` and SRC-111 decision-state CSS were removed from `NO_NEW_VALUE` and routed to bounded checker/package candidates; and
- SRC-003/SRC-004 now reconcile existing DEC-07/DEC-09 targets, while SRC-005 records the absent DEC-06 target and separate active-workspace owner candidate.

Post-repair state: `ACCEPTED_BY_REVIEWER_WITH_REPAIRS`. Independent re-audit confirmed all six blocker families resolved. No classification change authorizes adoption, execution, or source mutation.
